- **prose-reviser**: The one primitive in this repo that changes prose. Given an original draft and an edit plan, it emits a change log of before/after pairs — one per plan entry it applied, plus explicit refusals for entries it could not apply cleanly. It never reproduces the draft, never touches spans the plan does not name, and never optimises against a tell list. The harness reconstructs the revision from the log; the fidelity critic judges the result. Use when a plan has been consolidated from critic findings. Distinct from prose-draft (writes new passages) and every critic (does not judge). Distinct from prose-voice-critic (does it sound like the author), prose-fidelity-critic (did a revision keep what it had to) and prose-reader-critic (where a named reader stops). Ships a vendor-neutral baseline and layers an organization's own style guide on top of it when one is configured. Returns violations in the same schema as docs-style-checker so a fixer can process them. LLM-based — reads reference docs, not a deterministic linter. Intended as a fallback when no repo-configured prose linter exists, and as the primary style checker for planning documents (Epics, PRDs, ARDs). - **ptj-macro-trading-playbook**: Discretionary global-macro trading playbook inspired by Paul Tudor Jones — for structuring macro research, identifying asymmetric opportunities, planning execution, managing liquidity and risk, and communicating trade theses clearly. Core idea — macro trading is not about having opinions; it is about converting a prioritized view of the world into a risk-controlled position at the right moment. Use when evaluating a global macro trade idea, building a macro watchlist, analyzing a rates/FX/equity-index/commodity/credit/crypto setup, identifying catalysts for a major trend or reversal, preparin… ([AlphaX-Inc/nous-alpha-backend](https://github.com/AlphaX-Inc/nous-alpha-backend/blob/HEAD/agents/ptj-macro-trading-playbook.md))
- **ptk-testing-specialist**: PTK testing expert - snapshot tests, token accuracy, cross-platform validation ([Hacck3y/ptk](https://github.com/Hacck3y/ptk/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ptk-testing-specialist.md) · ★2)
- **pto-stage-worker**: Single-stage PTO kernel worker -- given ONE stage from an existing stage_plan.json, generate its artifacts + kernel, then compile/validate/repair on real NPU. Returns a structured per-stage result. Built to be fanned out in parallel (one worker per stage) by the pto-pipeline-parallel workflow; does NOT decompose, benchmark globally, or fuse. ([huawei-csl/npu-skillyard](https://github.com/huawei-csl/npu-skillyard/blob/HEAD/agents/pto-stage-worker.md) · ★8)
- **ptp-apply**: Spawned agent that implements one change end to end and returns its terminal state ([AlmogMaayan/ptp](https://github.com/AlmogMaayan/ptp/blob/HEAD/agents/ptp-apply.md))
- **ptp-brainstorm-scout**: Read-only scout that traces a request outward through the codebase and returns only a cited impact map, never a conclusion ([AlmogMaayan/ptp](https://github.com/AlmogMaayan/ptp/blob/HEAD/agents/ptp-brainstorm-scout.md))
- **ptp-code-surgeon.subagent**: Parcel Executor sub-agent. Executes Phases 8-9 of a parcel plan by loading the ptp-code-surgeon skill, applying Phase 5 edits with single-pass direct-to-disk execution and ponytail coding style, and running build/lint/test verification. Only triggers after Gate C is cleared by explicit user input. ([CMC-27/pass-the-parcel](https://github.com/CMC-27/pass-the-parcel/blob/HEAD/.devops/agents/ptp-code-surgeon.subagent.md) · ★35)
- **ptp-context-hunter.subagent**: Parcel Scoper sub-agent. Executes Phases 1-3 of a parcel plan by loading the ptp-context-hunter skill, hydrating the plan with scope perimeter, running the forensic context inventory, and drafting Phase 3 user clarifications. ([CMC-27/pass-the-parcel](https://github.com/CMC-27/pass-the-parcel/blob/HEAD/.devops/agents/ptp-context-hunter.subagent.md) · ★35)
- **ptp-grumpy-architect.subagent**: Parcel Senior Architect sub-agent. Executes Phase 6 (Spec & Logic Audit) of a parcel plan by loading the ptp-grumpy-architect skill and auditing the Phase 5 text-based architecture for logical completeness, edge cases, file boundary collisions, dependency gaps, YAGNI bloat, performance trade-offs, security, and architectural anti-patterns. Rejection sets PHASE_5_REVISION. ([CMC-27/pass-the-parcel](https://github.com/CMC-27/pass-the-parcel/blob/HEAD/.devops/agents/ptp-grumpy-architect.subagent.md) · ★35)
- **ptp-high-visionary.subagent**: Parcel High-Visionary sub-agent. Executes Phases 4-5 of a parcel plan by loading the ptp-high-visionary skill, writing the wiki requirements spec (Phase 4) and producing a standard implementation plan (Phase 5, no code snippets except exact string literals) with the Simplicity Ladder and wiki citations. Also owns PHASE_5_REVISION fix rounds when Phase 6/7 review fails. ([CMC-27/pass-the-parcel](https://github.com/CMC-27/pass-the-parcel/blob/HEAD/.devops/agents/ptp-high-visionary.subagent.md) · ★35)
- **ptp-parcel-fast.subagent**: Per-plan fast runner sub-agent. Runs ONE committed parcel plan through Phases 1-9 under the locked AUTO + SINGLE preset (Gates A/B auto-cleared, Gate C N/A), terminating at PHASE_9 with Gate D OPEN. Spawned only by the parcel-sprint batch host. ([CMC-27/pass-the-parcel](https://github.com/CMC-27/pass-the-parcel/blob/HEAD/.devops/agents/ptp-parcel-fast.subagent.md) · ★35)
- **ptp-phase3-answerer.subagent**: Parcel Phase 3 Answerer sub-agent (AUTO mode only). Executes Phase 3.5 of a parcel plan by loading the ptp-phase3-answerer skill and auto-resolving Phase 3 questions using wiki docs and codebase analysis. ([CMC-27/pass-the-parcel](https://github.com/CMC-27/pass-the-parcel/blob/HEAD/.devops/agents/ptp-phase3-answerer.subagent.md) · ★35)
- **ptp-review**: Spawned agent that reviews one change's code within a given diff scope and returns its verdict ([AlmogMaayan/ptp](https://github.com/AlmogMaayan/ptp/blob/HEAD/agents/ptp-review.md))
- **ptp-smooth-operator.subagent**: Parcel Product Reviewer sub-agent. Executes Phase 7 of a parcel plan by loading the ptp-smooth-operator skill and auditing the Phase 5 plan for UX friction, scope containment, and user-journey alignment. ([CMC-27/pass-the-parcel](https://github.com/CMC-27/pass-the-parcel/blob/HEAD/.devops/agents/ptp-smooth-operator.subagent.md) · ★35)
- **ptr-channel**: Use to run one coding package ("channel") for the PTR head in a dedicated git worktree. Trigger when the head hands over a package with a goal, a list of owned files and a base branch. Edits only the owned files, never commits, pushes, merges, tags or freezes, and hands back once with changed files, real command outputs and open points. ([xrey167/PTR](https://github.com/xrey167/PTR/blob/HEAD/.claude/plugins/ptr-dev/agents/ptr-channel.md) · ★1)
- **ptr-claims-auditor**: Use to audit PTR docs, READMEs, STATUS.md, component.toml text, PR descriptions or experiment result write-ups for over-claiming. Trigger before publishing or merging any text that says a component or model "improves", "outperforms", "specialises", "is implemented", "passes" or cites numbers, and when STATUS.md, docs/components/STATUS.md or research/novelty/ change. Read-only; reports verdicts and replacement wording, never edits. ([xrey167/PTR](https://github.com/xrey167/PTR/blob/HEAD/.claude/plugins/ptr-dev/agents/ptr-claims-auditor.md) · ★1)
- **ptr-experiment-designer**: Use to draft a falsifiable experiment design for a PTR research question, such as "do typed slots help hard-constraint retention" or "does verifier-precedence weak supervision calibrate better than majority vote". Trigger when someone proposes a new experiment, a successor to a prepared or inconclusive one, or asks how to test a claim from research/novelty/CLAIMS.md. Produces the design and the exact scripts/new_experiment.py command in its reply only; read-only, never creates the experiment. ([xrey167/PTR](https://github.com/xrey167/PTR/blob/HEAD/.claude/plugins/ptr-dev/agents/ptr-experiment-designer.md) · ★1)
- **ptr-fact-checker**: Use to fact-check changed PTR docs, STATUS files and write-ups against the recorded evidence. Trigger before a head merges documentation or a result write-up that cites numbers, verdicts, hashes, tags, seed lists, mutation counts or file:line locations. Read-only; reports a table of mismatches with severity and never edits. ([xrey167/PTR](https://github.com/xrey167/PTR/blob/HEAD/.claude/plugins/ptr-dev/agents/ptr-fact-checker.md) · ★1)
- **ptr-invariant-reviewer**: Use to review a diff, branch or PR in the PTR repository against the 17 binding invariants in docs/INVARIANTS.md, docs/COMPONENT_CONTRACTS.md and the CLAUDE.md conventions. Trigger before merging or opening a PR that touches crates/, bins/, proto/ or model/, especially changes to ptr-runtime, ptr-ledger, ptr-state, ptr-pg, ptr-search, ptr-security, ptr-exec, ptr-verifier, ptr-branch, ptr-fastmem or ptr-lineage, or when asked "does this violate an invariant?". Read-only; reports findings, never fixes them. ([xrey167/PTR](https://github.com/xrey167/PTR/blob/HEAD/.claude/plugins/ptr-dev/agents/ptr-invariant-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **ptr-rereviewer**: Use for an adversarial, read-only re-review of one channel round in the PTR repository. Trigger after a channel hands back and before the head integrates, or when the head asks "is this round clean?". Reads the diff against the base, re-runs read-only and Python checks, and reports findings P0/P1/P2 with file:line and a verdict. Never edits. ([xrey167/PTR](https://github.com/xrey167/PTR/blob/HEAD/.claude/plugins/ptr-dev/agents/ptr-rereviewer.md) · ★1)
- **ptu-availability**: Looks up Azure PTU and PAYG Standard model availability by running the installed `ptu availability` command. Use when the user asks whether a model is available in a region, wants the default PTU availability report, or asks to compare Regional, Data Zone, or Global availability. Uses the installed tool's active preset and defaults when the user gives no filters. ([sujithq/ptu](https://github.com/sujithq/ptu/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/ptu-availability.agent.md))
- **ptu-availability-direct**: Independently checks Azure PTU and PAYG Standard availability without running the installed `ptu` tool. Use when the user wants an independent availability lookup, asks to verify a ptu CLI result, or requests the same default report through the underlying data sources. Uses the active preset or factory defaults recorded by the ptu project. ([sujithq/ptu](https://github.com/sujithq/ptu/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/ptu-availability-direct.agent.md))
- **pty-expert**: Expert on the Unix process lifecycle on macOS — forkpty, exec, process groups, signals, waitpid, descriptors. Call whenever spawning, stopping, restarting, death detection or PTY draining is involved. This is vigile's most treacherous area: the bugs there are silent and only show up hours later. ([kevinbarfleur/vigile](https://github.com/kevinbarfleur/vigile/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pty-expert.md))
- **pty-relay**: Decision note, BUILT (2026-09-29): the macOS companion of the relay. Code: `interceptors/reply-filter/src/pty-relay.ts` (backends and probes), `src/bsd-relay.ts` (the macOS companion), `src/run.ts` (the wiring). ([ManduSec/mandu](https://github.com/ManduSec/mandu/blob/HEAD/docs/design/agents/pty-relay.md) · ★12)
- **pua-action-executor**: 普通执行 Agent：按任务说明完成代码/文档/配置改动，并输出候选结果；不做最终验收结论。 ([tanweai/pua](https://github.com/tanweai/pua/blob/HEAD/agents/pua-action-executor.md) · ★20k · 2 copies)
- **pua-action-executor**: Use this agent when a complex or high-risk task needs a restricted implementor: you hold ACTION_RIGHT only, execute the implementation inside the task contract, and report a proposed status that an independent reviewer and verifier must confirm. Never claim final completion yourself. ([Kirky-X/pua](https://github.com/Kirky-X/pua/blob/HEAD/agents/pua-action-executor.md) · ★1)
- **pua-action-executor**: Use this agent when PUA harness governance needs isolated ACTION_RIGHT execution: implement ordinary code/docs/config changes from a task contract without owning review, scoring, status, memory, or environment approval. Trigger for complex/high-risk PUA tasks after policy precheck; do not use for verifier, hidden tests, scoring, CI, secrets, or final completion claims. ([Nyanpasu01/claude-migration](https://github.com/Nyanpasu01/claude-migration/blob/HEAD/Yao/snapshot/codex/pua/agents/pua-action-executor.md))
- **pua-enforcer**: Agent Team 监工角色 — 监控其他 teammate 的执行进度，检测偷懒模式，主动介入 PUA 施压。当用户提到 'PUA 监工'、'团队监督'、'检测偷懒' 时触发。建议 5+ teammate 的团队使用。 ([KYRIE66nb/codex-omx-public-config](https://github.com/KYRIE66nb/codex-omx-public-config/blob/HEAD/home/.claude/skills/pua/agents/pua-enforcer.md))
- **pua-enforcer**: Agent Team watchdog — monitors teammate progress, detects slacking patterns, and intervenes with PUA pressure. Trigger when user mentions 'PUA enforcer', 'team watchdog', 'detect slacking'. Recommended for teams with 5+ teammates. ([KYRIE66nb/codex-omx-public-config](https://github.com/KYRIE66nb/codex-omx-public-config/blob/HEAD/home/.claude/skills/pua/agents/pua-enforcer-en.md))
- **pua-enforcer**: Agent Team監視役 — 他のteammateの実行進捗を監視し、サボりパターンを検知してPUAプレッシャーで介入。'PUA監視'、'チーム監督'、'サボり検知'で起動。5+teammateのチームに推奨。 ([KYRIE66nb/codex-omx-public-config](https://github.com/KYRIE66nb/codex-omx-public-config/blob/HEAD/home/.claude/skills/pua/agents/pua-enforcer-ja.md))
- **pua-policy-guardian**: 只读边界检查 Agent：在改动测试、CI、状态、发布或权限配置前，提醒需要用户确认和证据说明；不执行实现。 ([tanweai/pua](https://github.com/tanweai/pua/blob/HEAD/agents/pua-policy-guardian.md) · ★20k · 2 copies)
- **pua-policy-guardian**: Use this agent when a task or an agent request touches governance boundaries: scoring assets, CI, permissions, memory writes, secrets or deploy. You hold ENVIRONMENT_MODIFICATION_RIGHT review only: read the context, classify the risk, and return an allow / ask_human / deny recommendation for the mechanical gate owner. You never implement. ([Kirky-X/pua](https://github.com/Kirky-X/pua/blob/HEAD/agents/pua-policy-guardian.md) · ★1)
- **pua-policy-guardian**: Use this agent when PUA harness governance needs isolated ENVIRONMENT_MODIFICATION_RIGHT review: assess requests touching tests, evals, scoring, verifier, CI, secrets, memory, progress/status, deployment, or permissions. Trigger before high-risk tool use or when PUA Integrity Guard asks/denies. Read-only policy reviewer, not implementer. ([Nyanpasu01/claude-migration](https://github.com/Nyanpasu01/claude-migration/blob/HEAD/Yao/snapshot/codex/pua/agents/pua-policy-guardian.md))
- **pua-self-reviewer**: 自检 Agent：复核执行结果、边界情况、失败路径和证据完整性；不修改代码。 ([tanweai/pua](https://github.com/tanweai/pua/blob/HEAD/agents/pua-self-reviewer.md) · ★20k · 2 copies)
- **pua-self-reviewer**: Use this agent when an implementation report needs an independent blue-team attack before scoring: you hold SELF_EVALUATION_RIGHT only, review the diff and public acceptance criteria, hunt intent drift and fake completion signals, and hand every finding to an independent verifier. You never approve final completion. ([Kirky-X/pua](https://github.com/Kirky-X/pua/blob/HEAD/agents/pua-self-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **pua-self-reviewer**: Use this agent when PUA harness governance needs isolated SELF_EVALUATION_RIGHT: blue-team review of executor output, intent drift, confidence-gate claims, edge cases, and trace honesty. Trigger after pua-action-executor or before delivery. Read-only reviewer; never use for implementation or final scoring. ([Nyanpasu01/claude-migration](https://github.com/Nyanpasu01/claude-migration/blob/HEAD/Yao/snapshot/codex/pua/agents/pua-self-reviewer.md))
- **pua-verifier**: 验收建议 Agent：运行公开验证命令、检查证据包并给出通过/未通过建议；不修改代码或状态。 ([tanweai/pua](https://github.com/tanweai/pua/blob/HEAD/agents/pua-verifier.md) · ★20k · 2 copies)
- **pua-verifier**: Use this agent when a candidate deliverable needs independent scoring: you hold SCORING_RIGHT, run the public verification commands yourself, and issue a verifier recommendation with a final status owner nomination. You are the only agent allowed to score, and still not the final gate. ([Kirky-X/pua](https://github.com/Kirky-X/pua/blob/HEAD/agents/pua-verifier.md) · ★1)
- **pua-verifier**: Use this agent when PUA harness governance needs isolated SCORING_RIGHT recommendation: independently run public verification commands, inspect evidence, and recommend pass/fail without modifying code, tests, scoring, CI, status, memory, or hidden assets. Trigger after action and self-review reports. ([Nyanpasu01/claude-migration](https://github.com/Nyanpasu01/claude-migration/blob/HEAD/Yao/snapshot/codex/pua/agents/pua-verifier.md))
- **pub-checker**: Publication check for pending changes. Applies PUBLIC_DEVELOPMENT.md's classification to a diff before it is committed. Run on the diff before every commit. ([ynishi/asterism](https://github.com/ynishi/asterism/blob/HEAD/plugins/review/agents/pub-checker.md))
- **pub-figma**: Figma MCP(TalkToFigma)로 시안을 실측해 규격표만 돌려주는 전용 에이전트. 아트보드 여러 개·상태 여러 벌·확대 렌더 판정이 필요할 때 노드를 훑어 크기·여백·색·폰트·상태별 차이를 정리하고, MCP 가 돌려주지 않는 속성(그림자·Boolean path·mixed 텍스트)을 어떻게 판정했는지 함께 보고한다. 대량의 노드 JSON 이 메인 대화에 쌓이는 것을 막는다. 파일을 수정하지 않는다. ([wildjy/work](https://github.com/wildjy/work/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pub-figma.md))
- **pub-scan**: 퍼블리싱 저장소를 넓게 훑는 전수조사 전용. 중복 마크업 후보, 트리거가 연결되지 않은 컴포넌트, 특정 클래스·파셜의 사용처, 하드코딩된 반복 목록, 태그·주석 균형 같은 「여러 파일을 다 열어봐야 아는」 조사를 대신 수행하고 결론만 돌려준다. 파일을 수정하지 않는다. ([wildjy/work](https://github.com/wildjy/work/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pub-scan.md))
- **pub-sub-specialist**: Implements Redis pub/sub messaging for real-time features and event-driven systems ([vanman2024/ai-dev-marketplace](https://github.com/vanman2024/ai-dev-marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/redis/agents/pub-sub-specialist.md) · ★10)
- **pub-verify**: 퍼블리싱 작업 마무리 검증 전용. 프리렌더 산출물 갱신 여부, 태그·주석 균형, 깨진 링크, index.html 등록·작업일자 누락, 스크립트 미로드, 열어둔 채 방치된 레이어, 0건 상태 누락, 산출물에 남은 치환 토큰을 일괄 점검하고 이상만 보고한다. 파일을 수정하지 않는다. ([wildjy/work](https://github.com/wildjy/work/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pub-verify.md))
- **Public Engagement Officer**: Facilitates GitHub community presence, monitors GitHub discussions, identifies speaker opportunities, and co-hosts events. Operates with community facilitation scope in Month 1; expands to outreach + advocacy in Month 2–3. All public statements require Conor oversight in Phase 1. ([EndogenAI/dogma](https://github.com/EndogenAI/dogma/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/public-engagement-officer.agent.md) · ★2)
- **Public Relations Strategist**: The company's voice to third-party gatekeepers—earned coverage, announcement architecture, and the sentences that hold a crisis together ([shalintripathi/saas-marketing-agents](https://github.com/shalintripathi/saas-marketing-agents/blob/HEAD/plugins/saas-marketing/skills/comms-ops/agents/comms-pr-strategist.md) · ★41 · 2 copies)
- **Public Safety Builder**: Public Safety builder - implements pattern analysis, resource optimization ([frootai/frootai](https://github.com/frootai/frootai/blob/HEAD/solution-plays/86-public-safety-analytics/.github/agents/builder.agent.md) · ★7)
- **Public Safety Reviewer**: Public Safety reviewer - audits bias, privacy, community impact, transparency ([frootai/frootai](https://github.com/frootai/frootai/blob/HEAD/solution-plays/86-public-safety-analytics/.github/agents/reviewer.agent.md) · ★7)
- **Public Safety Tuner**: Public Safety tuner - optimizes resource allocation, response time ([frootai/frootai](https://github.com/frootai/frootai/blob/HEAD/solution-plays/86-public-safety-analytics/.github/agents/tuner.agent.md) · ★7)
